[08/20/2026, 18:35:29]
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 3. **Manually maintained pages**: `pages-hub/index.html`, `pages-hub/architecture.html`, `pages-hub/desig[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] n.html` — these are hand-curated.
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29]
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] So the update pipeline:
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 1. Rebas upstream
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 2. Tests
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 3. Reinstalls

[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 6. Commits regenerated artifacts
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] 7. Pushes to mirrors
[08/20/2026, 18:35:29]

[08/20/2026, 18:35:29] - Are the HTML pages accurate (version numbers, commit counts, feature lists)?

[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] # Job Scout — Outputs & Dashboard (companion folder)
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33]
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > **The main build of this folder is `job-scout-dashboard.html`.** One self-contained
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > file: 1,640 open roles (≤30 days; engine-synced 2026-06-23; tab + era counts computed live so they
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] never drift), era classification, company intel, salary-vs-market,
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > strategy memo, grove design system, embedded fonts. Open it in a browser — no server,
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > no dependencies. Everything else here either supports it (tests, qa, trackers) or is
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > an immutable session snapshot.
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] >
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > This folder is the **generated-outputs companion** described in
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > `career-hq/ECOSYSTEM.md` §6.12. The Job Scout *application/engine* is NOT here —
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] > it lives at `career-hq/job-scout/` and is built/maintained in Code Puppy sessions.
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33]
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] ## Layout (canonical)
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33]
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] | Path | What it is | Status |
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] |---|---|---|
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] | `job-scout-dashboard.html` | **THE dashboard.** Grove-skinned, 6 tabs (Overview / Matches / Compare /
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] All Jobs / Strategy / Method), era classifier verdicts, status tracking via localStorage | **Canonical —
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] main build** |
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] | `Job-Scout-Pipeline-Tracker.xlsx` | Canonical application-pipeline tracker (dropdowns, conditional
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] formatting, market deltas) | Canonical record of truth for application state |
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] | `Job-Scout-Tracker-GSheets.xlsx` | Sheets-safe export of the tracker for sharing | Share artifact —
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] re-export after major updates |
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] | `tests/` | QA suite: `run_qa.sh <round>` → static, well-formedness, data-integrity, DOM/functional,
[08/20/2026, 18:50:33] a11y (oklch contrast math) | Active — run before/after any dashboard edit |

[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] # Code Puppy Notes
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## Benton Drones Lead Ingest
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] A local-first lead-ingest MVP. Python stdlib + SQLite only. No Node, no npm, no external services
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] required.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## Launch
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```bash
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] make init-db        # create the SQLite database
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] make run            # start the server at http://127.0.0.1:8000
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Then visit:
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] - `http://127.0.0.1:8000/signup` — public signup form
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] - `http://127.0.0.1:8000/admin-login` — admin login (password via `ADMIN_PASSWORD` env var)
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] - `http://127.0.0.1:8000/admin` — admin dashboard
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] - `http://127.0.0.1:8000/landing-page.html` — standalone branded landing page
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## Test
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```bash
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] make test           # python -m unittest discover -s tests
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Tests use an in-memory or temp database — no `data/` directory needed.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## Extend
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ### Add a new route
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Edit `lead_ingest/server.py` → `do_GET` or `do_POST`, then add a handler method.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ### Add a new DB table
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Edit `lead_ingest/db.py` → `SCHEMA` string and add helper functions. `init_db` runs on every request so
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] new tables appear automatically.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ### Add a new test
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Drop a `test_*.py` file in `tests/` using `unittest.TestCase`.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## Optional dependencies
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] - `fpdf2>=2.8.0` — enables true PDF export at `/admin/lead/<id>/pdf`. Without it, the server falls back
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] to an HTML print view.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```bash
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] pip install -r requirements.txt   # or: pip install fpdf2
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ## JIRA integration
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] Set these env vars to enable JIRA ticket creation per signup:
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] ```bash
[08/20/2026, 18:51:05] JIRA_BASE_URL=https://your-domain.atlassian.net
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] JIRA_USER_EMAIL=you@example.com
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] JIRA_API_TOKEN=your-api-token

[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] JIRA_ISSUE_TYPE=Task
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ```
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] If any are missing, signups are queued in `jira_queue` for later processing.

[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] # Illuminating Concepts – Agent Guide
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ## Project goal
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] Rebuild https://illuminatingconcepts.com as a branded full-stack application that closely matches the
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] live site’s visual design and information architecture, while adding an interactive
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] infrastructure/architecture diagram and modern API-backed data layer.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] This guide explains what is already implemented, how the agents are expected to work, and what remains to
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] be done so future runs can pick up the work reliably.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ## Repository layout (current)
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] Root folder: `illuminating-concepts/`
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - `.venv/` – Python virtual environment with Flask installed.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - `app/` – Main Flask application package.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `__init__.py` – App factory and blueprint registration.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `db.py` – SQLite setup, schema bootstrapping, seed wiring, CLI commands.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `seed.py` – Seed data for services and ~47 portfolio projects (slug, name, location, category, year,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] size, services).
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `api.py` – JSON REST API blueprint (settings, services, projects, with filtering/sorting).
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `static/`
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]     - `css/main.css` – Rebuilt design system: fluid type scale, brand palette, layout rules for home,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] services/about, portfolio, contact, legal, architecture diagram page, footer, motion and print styles.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]     - `font.css` – Mirrored font-face declarations for `Gothamic-Book` (downloaded from live site) to
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] keep typography brand-faithful.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]     - `js/` – Reserved for client-side interactions (portfolio filters, worklist/diagram interactions,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] contact form behavior).
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]   - `templates/` – Jinja templates (base layout and individual pages) to be built out / completed.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - `instance/ic.db` – SQLite database created on first run via app bootstrap.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ## Current implementation status
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - Python 3.14 and a local virtual environment are configured; Flask 3.x is installed into `.venv`.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - A Flask app factory initializes the app, ensures the instance folder exists, and registers the `routes`
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] and `api` blueprints.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] - SQLite schema is defined in `db.py`, including tables for:
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] === job-scout - CODE-PUPPY-BRIEF ===
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] # Brief for Code Puppy — job-scout outputs companion
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] Paste this into a Code Puppy session working in `03-personal/job-scout/` or on
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] the engine at `career-hq/job-scout/`. Updated 2026-06-12 by Tyler's Cowork
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] session. Treat as authoritative for this folder.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ---
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] ## What this folder is (and is not)
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] **Generated-outputs companion** (per `career-hq/ECOSYSTEM.md` §6.12). The Job
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] Scout *engine* is NOT here — it lives at `career-hq/job-scout/` and is built in
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] Code Puppy sessions with its own 151-pytest gate. This folder holds what the
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] engine and Cowork sessions produce.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] **The main build is `job-scout-dashboard.html`** — one self-contained static
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] file (no server, no JS deps, subset fonts embedded): ~1,640 open roles ≤30 days (engine-backed,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] regenerated by scripts/sync_dashboard.py; synced 2026-06-23),
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] merged/de-duped from the engine's store (`career-hq/job-scout/data/jobs/*.json`,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] status ≠ stale) plus browser-session scrapes (LinkedIn, Indeed,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] careers.walmart.com — sources the key-free adapters can't reach). Six tabs
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] (Overview / Matches / Compare / All Jobs / Strategy / Method), 15-role curated
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] match set on an explained 6-dimension rubric, era classification per role
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] (ai / hyb / leg / std with matched-signal tooltips + CS-degree-gate detection),
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] company reputation/funding intel, posted-comp-vs-market deltas, recruiter
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] strategy memo. Design system inherited 1:1 from granlund-grove
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] (Fraunces/JetBrains embedded, oklch cedar/moss/spruce, glass/grain/lift,
[08/20/2026, 18:51:06] warm-paper light theme; moss = strong fit).
[08/20/2026, 18:51:16]

[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] For ALL projects:

[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] === KENNEL MEMORY - how it works ===

[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] # Puppy Kennel
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] Local-first memory for Code Puppy. Inspired by [MemKennel](https://github.com/MemKennel/memkennel)'s
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] wings → rooms → drawers model, but backed by **SQLite + FTS5** instead of
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] ChromaDB. No daemon, no API key, no cloud, multi-process safe via WAL mode.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] ## Why not just use MemKennel / Mem0 / Chroma?
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] We looked. Hard. The summary:
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] - **MemKennel** — Beautiful concepts, but ChromaDB's embedded `PersistentClient`
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   is not safe across multiple processes hitting the same kennel. Upstream issues
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   #1581, #948, #1646 are all flavors of "we need a daemon to make this work."
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   We run 20 puppies sometimes. A daemon is not in the cards.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] - **Mem0** — Open issue #4892: "concurrent AsyncMemory writes corrupt Qdrant
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   HNSW index." Same disease. Also requires an LLM API key just to *store*
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   memories, and phones home to PostHog by default. No thanks.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] - **ChromaDB / Qdrant / Faiss directly** — All ANN-index-backed; all multi-
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   process-hostile in embedded mode. ANN is only worth the trouble at >100K
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]   vectors. We're nowhere near that.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] SQLite has had production-grade full-text search (FTS5, BM25-ranked) since
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] 2015 and has been multi-process safe via WAL since 2010. We do not need
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] anything fancier.
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] ## Wing namespacing — compartmentalization without isolation walls
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] Three wing namespaces, all in one shared kennel:
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] | Wing | Example | Purpose |
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58]
[08/20/2026, 18:51:58] === JSON AGENT FORMAT ===

[08/20/2026, 18:52:18]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:18] 1. ALL FOUR projects — the foundation (do this first)
[08/20/2026, 18:52:18]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:19] Add an `AGENTS.md` to each project root. Code-puppy auto-reads it on every session. It's the single highe[08/20/2026, 18:52:19] st-leverage thing — it tells the agent the project's stack, conventions, test commands, and what not to t[08/20/2026, 18:52:19] ouch. No plugin needed, just a markdown file.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:19]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20] Use `--cwd` when resuming sessions. Launch with code-puppy --cwd (or type here in the /load_context picke[08/20/2026, 18:52:20] r, or Ctrl+T in the autosave picker). This filters sessions to just the current project instead of wading[08/20/2026, 18:52:20]  through every session you've ever had across all four projects.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20] 2. illuminating-concepts (Flask app, active dev, 13 dirty files)
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20] This is the most actively-developed project and the best candidate for project plugins.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:20]
[08/20/2026, 18:52:21] • Project plugin: `/run` and `/test` slash commands. A .code_puppy/plugins/flask-dev/register_callbacks.p[08/20/2026, 18:52:21] y that registers /run → flask run --debug and /test → python -m pytest. No more typing the full command.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:22] • Project agent: `ic-builder.json`. A JSON agent scoped to this repo with the Flask stack, SQLite schema,[08/20/2026, 18:52:22]  and template conventions baked into its system prompt. Tools: read_file, replace_in_file, agent_run_shel[08/20/2026, 18:52:22] l_command, list_files. This is the agent you delegate template tweaks to.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:22] • `model_select` hook. Route template/CSS tweaks to a cheaper model (Sonnet/Haiku), architecture work to [08/20/2026, 18:52:22] the expensive one. The hook reads the prompt and picks.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:22] • Kennel memory. Remember "we're rebuilding illuminatingconcepts.com, Flask 3.x, SQLite schema has these [08/20/2026, 18:52:22] tables, brand palette is X" so every new session doesn't start from scratch.
[08/20/2026, 18:52:22]
